## Angelic Chorus

[Source actor](https://github.com/foundryvtt/pf2e/blob/dfeaa4b119e8ee4a6a596915d5a2ed8684bb8a28/packs/pf2e/battlecry-bestiary/angelic-chorus.json). pf2e; level 12. Current library selection.

| Ability | Source economy | Disposition | Proposed game effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| +1 Status to All Saves vs. Magic | passive | proposed / magic-saves | Retain the source magic-save bonus as a conditional effect. The existing magic-ward signal carries no active benefit. |
| Harmonizing Aura | passive | proposed / sonic-aura | Preserve the ally/enemy distinction for sonic potency and auditory/sonic protection. Proposed scale: +1 to allies and -1 to enemies on relevant checks/defences; apply only while in range. |
| Admonishing Hymn | 2 action(s); see source for variable costs | extension / deafening-critical | Volley deafens on a critical hit. Add an auditory-reception condition through the target’s next activation; prevent auditory support and auditory attacks from treating it as a hearing target. This needs an auditory effect predicate. |
| Harmonized Spellcasting | passive | proposed / sonic-casting | Give sonic Cast attacks a distinct potency benefit; proposed +2 to the damage-resolving check instead of unscaled bonus wounds. Keep it on sonic spells only; needs spell damage tags. |
| Righteous Clamor | 1 action(s); see source for variable costs | baseline / base-damage | Retain the named damage profile, source action cost, save defence, damage types, range/area, and segment scaling. This description adds no automatic condition rider; apply separately reviewed passive and follow-up abilities only through explicit links. |

Common troop rules: Troop Defenses, Troop Movement. Their source text remains in the evidence; the review treats formation size and HP thresholds as an explicit abstraction.

Reactions: None in the source action items. See the separate reaction inventory.

Embedded spells: Zealous Conviction; Breath of Life; Truespeech (Constant); Cleanse Affliction (At Will); Clear Mind; Clear Mind (At Will); Noise Blast; Noise Blast; Noise Blast (At Will); Angelic Messenger; Counter Performance (At Will); Courageous Anthem; Heal; Heal; Uplifting Overture. Spell items and casting entries remain in the evidence; this review maps action-specific casting modifiers, rather than rewriting the spell catalogue.

## Angelic Host

[Source actor](https://github.com/foundryvtt/pf2e/blob/dfeaa4b119e8ee4a6a596915d5a2ed8684bb8a28/packs/pf2e/hells-destiny-bestiary/angelic-host.json). pf2e; level 20. Current library selection.

| Ability | Source economy | Disposition | Proposed game effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| Constant Spells | passive | reference / campaign-or-metadata | Retain the actual embedded spell list and constant effects. A marker alone neither identifies spells nor grants a new tradition; the current generic Cast repertoire needs a separate spell-access review. |
| Aura of Righteousness | passive | proposed / protective-aura | Give adjacent allies +2 Defence and saves against unholy enemies only. Retain holy/unholy predicates before enabling the aura. |
| Blessed Armaments | 1 action(s); see source for variable costs | baseline / base-damage | Retain the named damage profile, source action cost, save defence, damage types, range/area, and segment scaling. This description adds no automatic condition rider; apply separately reviewed passive and follow-up abilities only through explicit links. |
| Divine Lance Fusillade | 1 action(s); see source for variable costs | proposed / fast-volley | Keep Angelic Host’s Divine Lance Fusillade as a 1-action Volley profile with its holy/spirit tags. The same-name Divine Warden action costs 2; do not merge by name. |
| Troop Spellcasting | passive | proposed / healing-casting | Angelic Host’s Troop Spellcasting strengthens healing rather than widening areas. Proposed conversion: on a successful healing Cast, restore 1 extra Health within the recipient ceiling, once per activation. Keep this same-name variant distinct. |

Common troop rules: Troop Defenses, Troop Movement. Their source text remains in the evidence; the review treats formation size and HP thresholds as an explicit abstraction.

Reactions: None in the source action items. See the separate reaction inventory.

Embedded spells: Interplanar Teleport; Breath of Life; Truespeech (Constant); Dispel Magic; Heal; Heal; Heal; Heal. Spell items and casting entries remain in the evidence; this review maps action-specific casting modifiers, rather than rewriting the spell catalogue.

| Feint | 1 action(s); see source for variable costs | proposed / feint | Spend 1 action and check Deception against target Perception. Success grants +2 to this troop’s next attack against that target; critical success retains it through the activation. It does not expose the target to every ally. |
| Focus Gaze | 1 action(s); see source for variable costs | extension / focused-gaze | Spend 1 action to force the Wild Gaze save. A target already slowed by that gaze can become paralyzed on failure. Track the originating gaze, immunity until next activation, and an incapacitation limit. |
| Greensight | passive | reference / campaign-or-metadata | Retain the specific fog/foliage/smoke vision exemption. Apply it only to matching concealment once sight effects exist; it never ignores a solid wall. |

This description adds no automatic condition rider; apply separately reviewed passive and follow-up abilities only through explicit links. |
| Hunter's Link | passive | extension / shared-targeting | Allied ranged attacks within the source link radius ignore concealment. Requires concealment and a range-limited aura; it never grants sight through blocked terrain. |
| Infuse Arrow | 3 action(s); see source for variable costs | extension / spell-arrow | Spend 3 actions to combine one chosen eligible spell and a Volley. A miss consumes the spell; a hit delivers its touch/area effect, with the source save penalty for area delivery. Requires spell payload selection, resource spending, and compound resolution. |
| Instinctive Cooperation | passive | proposed / allied-effect-immunity | Improve saves against allied troop effects to critical success where a save exists. This can prevent friendly area damage; it neither heals nor prevents effects that offer no save. |
| Living Bow | passive | proposed / living-bow | Record the living bow’s magical and cold tags and unlimited ammunition for this troop only. The weapon’s numeric rune bonuses must not be added again to the already-derived Volley DC. |
| Living Bow Volley [Salvo] | 2 action(s); see source for variable costs | baseline / base-damage | Retain the named damage profile, source action cost, save defence, damage types, range/area, and segment scaling. This description adds no automatic condition rider; apply separately reviewed passive and follow-up abilities only through explicit links. |
| Planar Acclimation | passive | reference / campaign-or-metadata | Retain this communication, provisioning, storage, environmental, or occupational ability in the creature record. It adds no extra Attack or Volley effect under the current battle model. |
| Primal Magic | 2 action(s); see source for variable costs | extension / primal-table | Retain the source 1d10 table as a 2-action Will-targeting activity: slow, fear, electricity plus exposure, sickness, or casting impairment. Keep its non-routed prerequisite and seeded randomness; do not reduce it to a generic Blast. |
| Seeking Shots | passive | proposed / seeking-attacks | Ignore concealment for this troop’s ranged attacks and spell attacks. Keep sight and line-of-effect checks; cover and solid obstacles remain separate. |
| Supernatural Attacks | passive | proposed / critical-weakening | The Battle or Salvo attack weakens physical attacks on a critical hit until the target’s next activation begins. Preserve the source short expiry; do not make this permanent disorder. |
| Swift Recovery | passive | proposed / condition-recovery | At activation start, roll the source DC 15 flat check; success clears one eligible condition. Show the choice/priority explicitly and avoid removing campaign wounds or permanent Morale as if they were temporary conditions. |
| Troop Spellcasting | passive | source-question / source-integrity | Troop Spellcasting references pf2e-creature-crispr localization, absent from the PF2e checkout. Preserve the unresolved key and actual spell items; do not infer its rule from a same-name PF2e ability. |
| Wild Gaze | passive | proposed / slowing-gaze | At an enemy activation’s end in range, use the source Will save to remove one action (two on critical failure) next activation. Allow the source 1-action toggle. Its fear trait does not make the mechanical effect frightened. |
